import { createDb } from "@my-app/db";
import { migrateDatabase } from "@my-app/db/migrate";

import { env } from "#src/env.ts";

const db = createDb(env.DATABASE_URL);

await migrateDatabase(db);

await db.$client.promise().end();

import { existsSync } from "node:fs";

import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit";

const serverEnvFile = "../../apps/server/.env";

if (existsSync(serverEnvFile)) {
  process.loadEnvFile(serverEnvFile);
}

export default defineConfig({
  dbCredentials: { url: process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "" },
  dialect: "mysql",
  out: "./src/migrations",
  schema: "./src/schema",
});

import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit";

const appDir = "../../apps/server";
const appEnvFile = `${appDir}/.env`;

if (existsSync(appEnvFile)) {
  process.loadEnvFile(appEnvFile);
}

export default defineConfig({
  dbCredentials: {
    url: path.resolve(appDir, process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "local.db"),
  },
  dialect: "sqlite",
  out: "./src/migrations",
  schema: "./src/schema",
});

import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit";

const appDir = "../../apps/web";
const appEnvFile = `${appDir}/.env`;

if (existsSync(appEnvFile)) {
  process.loadEnvFile(appEnvFile);
}

export default defineConfig({
  dbCredentials: {
    url: path.resolve(appDir, process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "local.db"),
  },
  dialect: "sqlite",
  out: "./src/migrations",
  schema: "./src/schema",
});

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mysql-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mysql-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mysql-expo-docker-e2e
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/mysql2";
import { createPool } from "mysql2";

import { relations } from "#src/relations.ts";

// drizzle-orm sets options on `client.config` and wraps the pool with `promise()`, which a `mysql2/promise` pool
// lacks. Close it with `db.$client.promise().end()`.
export const createDb = (url: string) =>
  drizzle({ client: createPool({ uri: url }), relations });

export type Database = ReturnType<typeof createDb>;

import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js";

import { relations } from "#src/relations.ts";

export const createDb = (url: string) =>
  drizzle({
    connection: { connection: { client_min_messages: "warning" }, url },
    relations,
  });

export type Database = ReturnType<typeof createDb>;

import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/node-sqlite";

import { relations } from "#src/relations.ts";

export const createDb = (path: string) => {
  const db = drizzle({ connection: { path, timeout: 5000 }, relations });
  db.$client.exec("pragma journal_mode = wal");

  return db;
};

export type Database = ReturnType<typeof createDb>;

# packages/db/src/index.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { PgClient } from "@effect/sql-pg";
import * as PgDrizzle from "drizzle-orm/effect-postgres";
import type { Effect } from "effect";
import { Config, Context, Layer } from "effect";

import { relations } from "#src/relations.ts";

const make = PgDrizzle.makeWithDefaults({ relations });

export class Database extends Context.Service<
  Database,
  Effect.Success<typeof make>
>()("@my-app/db/Database") {
  static readonly layer = Layer.effect(Database, make);

  static readonly layerConfig = Database.layer.pipe(
    Layer.provide(
      PgClient.layerConfig({ url: Config.Redacted("DATABASE_URL") })
    )
  );
}

import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/mysql2";
import { createPool } from "mysql2";

import { relations } from "#src/relations.ts";

// drizzle-orm sets options on `client.config` and wraps the pool with `promise()`, which a `mysql2/promise` pool
// lacks. Close it with `db.$client.promise().end()`.
export const createDb = (url: string) =>
  drizzle({
    client: createPool({ uri: url, disableEval: true, connectionLimit: 1 }),
    relations,
  });

export type Database = ReturnType<typeof createDb>;

import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { migrate } from "drizzle-orm/node-sqlite/migrator";

import type { Database } from "#src/index.ts";

const migrationsFolder = fileURLToPath(new URL("migrations", import.meta.url));

export const migrateDatabase = (db: Database) => {
  migrate(db, { migrationsFolder });
};

# packages/db/src/migrate.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { migrate } from "drizzle-orm/effect-postgres/migrator";
import { Effect } from "effect";

import { Database } from "#src/index.ts";

// The server bundle copies the folder beside `dist/migrate.mjs`, so this resolves from source and from the bundle.
const migrationsFolder = fileURLToPath(new URL("migrations", import.meta.url));

export const migrateDatabase = Effect.gen(function* () {
  const db = yield* Database;

  yield* migrate(db, { migrationsFolder });
}).pipe(Effect.withSpan("migrateDatabase"));

#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { defineRelations } from "drizzle-orm";

import { account, session, user, verification } from "#src/schema/auth.ts";

export const relations = defineRelations(
  { account, session, user, verification },
  (r) => ({
    account: {
      user: r.one.user({ from: r.account.userId, to: r.user.id }),
    },
    session: {
      user: r.one.user({ from: r.session.userId, to: r.user.id }),
    },
    user: {
      accounts: r.many.account(),
      sessions: r.many.session(),
    },
  })
);

import { defineRelations } from "drizzle-orm";

import { account, session, user, verification } from "#src/schema/auth.ts";
import { todos } from "#src/schema/todos.ts";

export const relations = defineRelations(
  { account, session, todos, user, verification },
  (r) => ({
    account: {
      user: r.one.user({ from: r.account.userId, to: r.user.id }),
    },
    session: {
      user: r.one.user({ from: r.session.userId, to: r.user.id }),
    },
    todos: {
      user: r.one.user({ from: r.todos.userId, to: r.user.id }),
    },
    user: {
      accounts: r.many.account(),
      sessions: r.many.session(),
      todos: r.many.todos(),
    },
  })
);

import {
  boolean,
  index,
  snakeCase,
  text,
  timestamp,
  varchar,
} from "drizzle-orm/mysql-core";

// MySQL indexes a bounded column only, so ids, keys, and lookups are `varchar`.
const id = () => varchar({ length: 36 });

const timestamps = {
  createdAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 })
    .notNull()
    .defaultNow()
    .$onUpdate(() => new Date()),
};

export const user = snakeCase.table("user", {
  email: varchar({ length: 255 }).notNull().unique(),
  emailVerified: boolean().notNull().default(false),
  id: id().primaryKey(),
  image: text(),
  name: text().notNull(),
  ...timestamps,
});

export const session = snakeCase.table(
  "session",
  {
    expiresAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 }).notNull(),
    id: id().primaryKey(),
    ipAddress: text(),
    token: varchar({ length: 255 }).notNull().unique(),
    userAgent: text(),
    userId: id()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("session_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)]
);

export const account = snakeCase.table(
  "account",
  {
    accessToken: text(),
    accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 }),
    accountId: text().notNull(),
    id: id().primaryKey(),
    idToken: text(),
    password: text(),
    providerId: text().notNull(),
    refreshToken: text(),
    refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 }),
    scope: text(),
    userId: id()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("account_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)]
);

export const verification = snakeCase.table(
  "verification",
  {
    expiresAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 }).notNull(),
    id: id().primaryKey(),
    identifier: varchar({ length: 255 }).notNull(),
    value: text().notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("verification_identifier_idx").on(table.identifier)]
);

import {
  boolean,
  index,
  snakeCase,
  text,
  timestamp,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

const timestamps = {
  createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true })
    .notNull()
    .defaultNow()
    .$onUpdate(() => new Date()),
};

export const user = snakeCase.table("user", {
  email: text().notNull().unique(),
  emailVerified: boolean().notNull().default(false),
  id: text().primaryKey(),
  image: text(),
  name: text().notNull(),
  ...timestamps,
});

export const session = snakeCase.table(
  "session",
  {
    expiresAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    id: text().primaryKey(),
    ipAddress: text(),
    token: text().notNull().unique(),
    userAgent: text(),
    userId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("session_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)]
);

export const account = snakeCase.table(
  "account",
  {
    accessToken: text(),
    accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
    accountId: text().notNull(),
    id: text().primaryKey(),
    idToken: text(),
    password: text(),
    providerId: text().notNull(),
    refreshToken: text(),
    refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
    scope: text(),
    userId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("account_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)]
);

export const verification = snakeCase.table(
  "verification",
  {
    expiresAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    id: text().primaryKey(),
    identifier: text().notNull(),
    value: text().notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("verification_identifier_idx").on(table.identifier)]
);

import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { index, integer, snakeCase, text } from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";

const now = sql`(cast(unixepoch('subsec') * 1000 as integer))`;

const timestamp = () => integer({ mode: "timestamp_ms" });

const timestamps = {
  createdAt: timestamp().notNull().default(now),
  updatedAt: timestamp()
    .notNull()
    .default(now)
    .$onUpdate(() => new Date()),
};

export const user = snakeCase.table("user", {
  email: text().notNull().unique(),
  emailVerified: integer({ mode: "boolean" }).notNull().default(false),
  id: text().primaryKey(),
  image: text(),
  name: text().notNull(),
  ...timestamps,
});

export const session = snakeCase.table(
  "session",
  {
    expiresAt: timestamp().notNull(),
    id: text().primaryKey(),
    ipAddress: text(),
    token: text().notNull().unique(),
    userAgent: text(),
    userId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("session_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)]
);

export const account = snakeCase.table(
  "account",
  {
    accessToken: text(),
    accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp(),
    accountId: text().notNull(),
    id: text().primaryKey(),
    idToken: text(),
    password: text(),
    providerId: text().notNull(),
    refreshToken: text(),
    refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp(),
    scope: text(),
    userId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("account_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)]
);

export const verification = snakeCase.table(
  "verification",
  {
    expiresAt: timestamp().notNull(),
    id: text().primaryKey(),
    identifier: text().notNull(),
    value: text().notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (table) => [index("verification_identifier_idx").on(table.identifier)]
);

import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  boolean,
  check,
  index,
  int,
  snakeCase,
  timestamp,
  varchar,
} from "drizzle-orm/mysql-core";

import { user } from "#src/schema/auth.ts";

export const todos = snakeCase.table(
  "todos",
  {
    completed: boolean().notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 }).notNull().defaultNow(),
    id: int().autoincrement().primaryKey(),
    title: varchar({ length: 200 }).notNull(),
    userId: varchar({ length: 36 })
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  },
  (table) => [
    index("todos_user_id_idx").on(table.userId),
    check(
      "todos_title_length",
      sql`char_length(trim(${table.title})) between 1 and 200`
    ),
  ]
);

export type Todo = typeof todos.$inferSelect;

import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  boolean,
  check,
  int,
  snakeCase,
  timestamp,
  varchar,
} from "drizzle-orm/mysql-core";

export const todos = snakeCase.table(
  "todos",
  {
    completed: boolean().notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: timestamp({ fsp: 3 }).notNull().defaultNow(),
    id: int().autoincrement().primaryKey(),
    title: varchar({ length: 200 }).notNull(),
  },
  (table) => [
    check(
      "todos_title_length",
      sql`char_length(trim(${table.title})) between 1 and 200`
    ),
  ]
);

export type Todo = typeof todos.$inferSelect;

import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  boolean,
  check,
  index,
  integer,
  snakeCase,
  text,
  timestamp,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

import { user } from "#src/schema/auth.ts";

export const todos = snakeCase.table(
  "todos",
  {
    completed: boolean().notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
    id: integer().primaryKey().generatedAlwaysAsIdentity(),
    title: text().notNull(),
    userId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  },
  (table) => [
    index("todos_user_id_idx").on(table.userId),
    check(
      "todos_title_length",
      sql`char_length(btrim(${table.title})) between 1 and 200`
    ),
  ]
);

export type Todo = typeof todos.$inferSelect;

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  check,
  index,
  integer,
  snakeCase,
  text,
} from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";

import { user } from "#src/schema/auth.ts";

export const todos = snakeCase.table(
  "todos",
  {
    completed: integer({ mode: "boolean" }).notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: integer({ mode: "timestamp_ms" })
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`(cast(unixepoch('subsec') * 1000 as integer))`),
    id: integer().primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    title: text().notNull(),
    userId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  },
  (table) => [
    index("todos_user_id_idx").on(table.userId),
    check(
      "todos_title_length",
      sql`length(trim(${table.title})) between 1 and 200`
    ),
  ]
);

export type Todo = typeof todos.$inferSelect;

  "todos",
  {
    completed: integer({ mode: "boolean" }).notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: integer({ mode: "timestamp_ms" })
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`(cast(unixepoch('subsec') * 1000 as integer))`),

# packages/db/src/schema/todos.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  boolean,
  check,
  index,
  integer,
  snakeCase,
  text,
  timestamp,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

import { user } from "#src/schema/auth.ts";

export const todos = snakeCase.table(
  "todos",
  {
    completed: boolean().notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
    id: integer().primaryKey().generatedAlwaysAsIdentity(),
    title: text().notNull(),
    userId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  },
  (table) => [
    index("todos_user_id_idx").on(table.userId),
    check(
      "todos_title_length",
      sql`char_length(btrim(${table.title})) between 1 and 200`
    ),
  ]
);
